Result: 在13个VLM上的实验揭示了模型间显著的Coverage差距、清晰的效能-效率权衡，以及稀疏或基于重叠的评估方法常常遗漏的失败模式。CAPProbe将开放式的标量评分转换为结构化的多选题阅读，降低了开放式评分偏差，同时保持评判员条件性，在固定阅读器下产生相对稳定的模型排名。

Conclusion: CapProbe提供了一种成本效益高、区域对齐、密度高的详细图像描述评估方案，能够更精确地捕捉VLM在详细描述生成中的事实准确性。基准数据、标注和评估代码将公开发布。

Abstract: Evaluating detailed image captions from Vision-Language Models (VLMs) requires going beyond surface-level semantic similarity. Reference-based metrics (e.g., CIDEr and SPICE) and LLM-as-scorer protocols struggle to verify dense factual claims, while existing QA-based alternatives generally offer lower probe density, narrower domain coverage, or no explicit alignment between individual questions and segmented image regions. We introduce CapProbe, a full-scene dense QA benchmark that turns detailed caption evaluation into region-aligned factual checking. Each image is decomposed into coarse semantic regions covering both foreground and background elements; for every retained region, we generate multiple-choice questions spanning 10 semantic categories, forming a dense checklist of probed visual facts. Guided by a two-tier taxonomy of 37 L1 domains and 219 L2 sub-domains, CapProbe comprises 346 images, 1,868 regions, and 25,650 questions, averaging 74 QA pairs per image. A language judge answers from the caption alone; an Uncertain option and Effective Accuracy provide a judge-dependent proxy for distinguishing unanswered probes from incorrectly resolved ones, while density-based metrics penalize verbose yet uninformative captions. The protocol is cost-effective: by converting unconstrained scalar scoring into structured MCQ reading, it reduces open-ended scoring bias while remaining judge-conditioned and yields relatively stable model rankings under a fixed reader. Experiments on 13 VLMs show large Coverage gaps across models, a clear competency-efficiency trade-off, and failure modes that sparse or overlap-based evaluation often misses. The benchmark data, annotations, and evaluation code will be released soon.

</details>


### [106] [Static in Frames, Dynamic in Events: Rethinking Features in Event Cameras as Motion Cues](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11075)
*Hesam Araghi,Jan van Gemert,Nergis Tomen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文分析事件相机中用于角点检测的两个特征（结构张量特征值和时空密度值），论证它们本质上是运动线索，并将其与局部几何信息结合以增强运动估计任务。实验表明，在合成数据集上该特征扩展具有互补性且鲁棒，在真实DSEC基准上加入这些特征可一致提升光流网络精度，尤其在数据稀缺和低容量模型场景下收益最大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 事件相机以异步方式捕获强度变化，具有高时间分辨率，需要新的预处理方法。与静态强度快照不同，事件数据天然编码了场景动态和物体运动信息，因此从事件派生的特征可能表现出基于帧视觉中无法直接类比的行为。本文旨在分析这些特征的运动线索本质，并将其应用于运动估计任务。

Method: 首先从理论上分析运动角点处结构张量特征值与运动方向的关系；然后在合成数据集上设计受控实验，验证在局部几何特征中扩展特征值和密度值能提供互补的运动信息，且对纹理和散粒噪声具有鲁棒性；最后将所提特征集成到最先进的基于事件的光流网络中，在真实DSEC基准上进行评估。

Result: 在真实DSEC基准上，加入所提特征一致地提升了光流网络精度，其中在数据稀缺场景和低容量模型上获得最大增益；在合成数据集上验证了特征的互补性和对纹理及噪声的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 结构张量特征值和时空密度值本质上是运动线索，将其与局部几何信息结合可以有效增强事件相机的运动估计任务，尤其适合数据稀缺和模型容量受限的场景。

Abstract: Event cameras capture intensity changes asynchronously with high temporal resolution, requiring novel preprocessing methods for downstream tasks. Unlike static intensity snapshots, event data inherently encode information about scene dynamics and object motion, meaning that features derived from events can exhibit behaviors with no direct analogue in frame-based vision. In this paper, we analyze two features used in event-based corner detection---the eigenvalues of the structure tensor and the spatiotemporal density values---and show that they are \emph{motion cues}. We hypothesize that these features, combined with local geometric information, can enhance motion estimation tasks. To validate this, we first theoretically analyze how the eigenvalues of the structure tensor at moving corner points relate to the direction of motion. We then design controlled experiments on a synthetic dataset, confirming that extending local geometric features with eigenvalues and density values provides complementary motion information and is robust to texture and shot noise. Finally, we integrate the proposed features into a state-of-the-art event-based optical flow network and evaluate on the real-world DSEC benchmark, where the added features consistently improve accuracy, with the largest gains in data-scarce scenarios and for lower-capacity models. The code for this paper can be found at: \href{https://github.com/hesamaraghi/static-in-frames-dynamic-in-events}{https://github.com/hesamaraghi/static-in-frames-dynamic-in-events}.

</details>


### [107] [Foundation Model-Enabled Efficient Data Sampling (FEEDS): A label-efficient training strategy for pan-cancer, multi-tracer PET/CT datasets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11076)
*Biratal Raj Wagle,Bashirul Azam Biswas,Grant Chau,Matthew E. Maeder,Muhammad Azeem Arshad,Michael S. Leapman,James B. Yu,Indrani Bhattacharya*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FEEDS是一种基于视觉基础模型嵌入的标签和计算高效学习策略，用于全身PET/CT图像中的自动病灶分割。它通过选择最具信息量和多样性的未标注病例进行专家标注，仅需有限且有代表性的训练集即可匹配全标注训练性能，同时减少70%的标注负担，并能在FDG和PSMA示踪剂及多种疾病中实现良好泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全身PET/CT的自动病灶分割对癌症检测、分期和治疗规划具有重要意义，但训练高精度分割模型需要大规模标注数据集，而标注过程耗时且需要专业知识。有限的标注数据导致模型准确性和泛化能力不足，难以满足临床需求。

Method: FEEDS利用视觉基础模型嵌入来选择最具信息量和多样性的未标注病例进行专家标注，是一种单步训练范式，仅需有限的代表性训练集，无需迭代式的主动学习或半监督学习流程。在AutoPET-III数据集上训练和验证，并在AutoPET-III、DeepPSMA和内部Dartmouth-Hitchcock医学中心数据集三个独立测试集上进行评估。

Result: FEEDS在体素、病灶和解剖区域水平上优于随机采样标注、伪标签半监督学习及仅用有限标注数据训练的方法。它在三个测试集上均表现出良好的泛化能力，覆盖FDG和PSMA两种示踪剂及多种疾病，以70%更少的标注负担达到全标注（100%）训练的性能水平。

Conclusion: FEEDS有效解决了自动病灶分割框架中的标签稀缺问题，为从大型未标注临床数据仓库构建具有代表性和多样性的标注队列提供了实用方案，兼顾了标注效率和模型性能。

Abstract: Automated lesion segmentation in whole-body PET/CT imaging can assist clinicians with cancer detection, staging, and treatment planning across radiotracers and cancer types. However, training lesion segmentation models that capture variations in lesion size, distribution, and appearance requires large annotated datasets, whose creation is both time- and expertise-intensive. As a result, models trained on limited labeled PET/CT data often lack the accuracy and generalizability needed for clinical use. We present FEEDS (Foundation model-Enabled Efficient Data Sampling), a label- and compute-efficient learning strategy that uses vision foundation model embeddings to select the most informative and diverse unlabeled cases for expert annotation. Unlike unsupervised, semi-supervised, and active learning approaches, FEEDS is a one-step training paradigm requiring only a limited, representative training set, making it label- and compute-efficient. We train and validate FEEDS using the AutoPET-III dataset. We test its accuracy and generalizability on three held-out sets: AutoPET-III, DeepPSMA, and an internal Dartmouth-Hitchcock Medical Center dataset. We evaluate clinical utility at the voxel, lesion, and anatomic region level to assess performance in high-risk areas and treatment planning utility. FEEDS outperforms random-sampling-based labeling, pseudolabel-based semi-supervised learning, and training with limited labeled data alone. It generalizes across all three test sets, FDG and PSMA tracers, and multiple diseases, matching fully-labeled (100\%) training performance with 70\% less annotation burden. FEEDS addresses the challenge of label scarcity in an automatic lesion segmentation framework by providing a practical approach for constructing representative and diverse annotation queues from large, unannotated clinical repositories.

</details>


### [108] [Learning Gaussian Structure: Intervention-Guided Density Control for Feed-Forward Driving Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11077)
*Hang Li,Jiahe Li,Meiying Gu,Jin Zheng,Lina Yu,Xiao Bai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出LGS框架，同时增强高斯结构和原始属性，通过学习稠密化策略在推理时直接调整高斯结构，并设计跨时间点查询显式聚合多时间戳特征，在Waymo和PandaSet数据集上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LiDAR的前馈高斯重建方法仅保留初始对应关系，无法像优化式3DGS那样通过训练梯度累积来决定场景表示的稠密化；且共享稀疏骨干网络仅隐式融合不同时间戳的观测，缺乏对单个原始体的显式跨时间证据聚合。

Method: 1) 提出高斯稠密化策略（Gaussian Densify Policy），基于局部梯度响应变化来学习稠密化图（包含剪枝分数和添加分数），在推理时直接调整高斯结构；2) 设计紧凑的跨时间点查询（Cross-Time Point Query），显式检索并聚合其他时间戳高斯原始体的邻近特征，用于可靠的属性预测。

Result: 在Waymo Open Dataset和PandaSet上的大量实验表明，LGS持续优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 通过显式的结构稠密化策略和跨时间特征聚合，LGS弥补了现有前馈高斯重建方法在结构优化和跨时间信息利用方面的不足，显著提升了重建质量。

Abstract: Feed-forward Gaussian reconstruction has recently emerged as an efficient approach for driving scene reconstruction. However, prevailing LiDAR-based methods preserve the initial correspondence between observed points and Gaussian primitives, treating the initialized primitive set as the final representation. Unlike optimization-based 3DGS, these methods cannot accumulate gradients during training to determine how the scenes representation should be densified. Meanwhile, the shared sparse backbone only fuses observations from different timestamps implicitly, without explicitly aggregating cross-time evidence for individual primitives. In this paper, we present Learning Gaussian Structure (LGS), a framework that enhances both Gaussian structure and primitive attributes. Our key observation is that changes in local gradient responses induced by a prune or add intervention reveal whether the corresponding structural adjustment benefits reconstruction. Based on this observation, our Gaussian Densify Policy learns a Densify Map comprising Prune and Addition Scores from controlled interventions, and directly adjusts the Gaussian structure during inference. We further develop a compact Cross-Time Point Query that explicitly retrieves and aggregates neighboring features from Gaussian primitives at other timestamps for reliable attribute prediction. Extensive experiments on the Waymo Open Dataset and PandaSet demonstrate that LGS consistently outperforms existing methods.

</details>


### [109] [Every Packet Counts: Dispersing Information for Loss-Resilient Learned Image Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11096)
*Yuhang Wei,Chuqin Zhou,Yibo Shi,Jing Wang,Guo Lu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种端到端的抗丢包图像压缩方案，通过通道间能量重分配（ICR）、交错通道分组（ICG）和两层双分支自回归结构，同时解决了包化阶段信息分布不均和熵编码阶段顺序解码依赖两个问题，在20%丢包率下PSNR比现有方法提升1.84dB，且方差降低一个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有学习型图像压缩方法在卫星和应急通信等场景下对丢包高度脆弱，根源在于包化阶段非均匀的信息分布以及熵编码阶段顺序解码的强依赖关系，这两大问题尚未被同时有效解决。

Method: 方法包含三个核心组件：1）ICR机制在包化前对通道能量进行重分配，防止关键信息集中于少数通道；2）ICG策略以交错方式对潜变量通道进行分组，将信息分散到各数据包中并控制包大小；3）采用两层双分支自回归结构缩短解码依赖链，限制丢包引起的错误级联传播。

Result: 在20%丢包率下，平均PSNR较LossResilientLIC提升1.84dB，PSNR方差降低一个数量级。仅在均匀随机丢包下训练的模型，能够泛化到Gilbert-Elliott信道建模的突发丢包场景，且优于专门针对该条件训练的现有方法。

Conclusion: 该方案通过同时解决信息分布均匀性和解码依赖链两个关键瓶颈，实现了更强的抗丢包韧性，并展现出对突发丢包场景的良好泛化能力，为实际通信场景中的可靠图像传输提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Learned image compression (LIC) has achieved impressive rate-distortion performance. However, existing methods remain highly vulnerable to packet loss, a common challenge in satellite and emergency communications. This vulnerability stems from non-uniform information distribution at the packetization stage and sequential decoding dependencies at the entropy coding stage. We propose an end-to-end loss-resilient image compression scheme that addresses both. Before packetization, we introduce an Inter-Channel Redistribution (ICR) mechanism to redistribute channel energy, preventing critical information concentrating in a small subset of channels. Then, an Interleaved Channel Grouping (ICG) strategy partitions latent channels in a strided manner to disperse information across packets, with each packet kept within constrained sizes. To limit cascading errors from lost packets, we adopt a two-layer dual-branch autoregressive structure to shorten the dependency chain. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method consistently outperforms existing approaches in both reconstruction quality and stability. At 20% packet loss, it achieves an average PSNR gain of 1.84 dB over LossResilientLIC while reducing PSNR variance by an order of magnitude. Notably, trained under uniform random loss only, our model generalizes to bursty loss modeled by the Gilbert-Elliott channel, outperforming methods explicitly trained for such conditions.

</details>


### [110] [AlbumentationsX: One Augmentation Pipeline for Images and Related Annotations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11123)
*Vladimir Iglovikov*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AlbumentationsX是一个数据增强库，通过在单个Compose对象中统一管理变换列表、概率、标注设置和随机种子，确保图像及其所有标注（掩码、边界框、关键点、立体视图、视频帧或体积）使用相同的随机变化，避免数据错位问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统数据增强库中，图像和标注可能接收不同的随机变化（如裁剪坐标不一致），导致训练数据被静默破坏，影响模型训练质量。

Method: 将所有变换逻辑（列表、概率、标注设置、随机种子）集中在一个Compose对象中，每次调用仅生成一次随机值并应用于训练样本的所有部分；将对象的掩码、边界框和标签绑定在一起，支持自定义变换、保存流程定义、回放单次调用过程。

Result: 通过实例展示了Compose在文件解码为数组之后、PyTorch批处理之前的工作流程，能够正确执行声明的变换；同时强调实践者仍需自行判断翻转、裁剪等操作是否保持标签语义正确。

Conclusion: AlbumentationsX通过统一随机源和结构化存储标注，解决了多模态数据增强中的对齐问题，为深度学习训练提供更可靠的数据预处理方案。

Abstract: Augmentation can corrupt a training example when an image and its annotations receive different random changes. A crop must use the same coordinates for the image, mask, boxes, keypoints, stereo views, video frames, or volume. Code paths that choose these values separately can silently misalign the data.
  AlbumentationsX keeps the transform list, probabilities, annotation settings, and random seed in one Compose object. Each call chooses random values once and applies them to every supported part of the training example. The library keeps each object's mask, box, and label together and lets projects add their own transforms. It can also save the pipeline definition, show what happened in one call, and run that call again.
  The examples place Compose after files have been decoded into arrays and before PyTorch groups examples into a batch. AlbumentationsX executes the declared transforms. Practitioners still decide whether a flip, crop, color change, or other operation preserves the correct label for their task.

</details>


### [111] [Is There Really a Camouflaged Object? Towards Realistic Camouflaged Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11135)
*Huafeng Chen,Yueming Lyu,Chenyang Si,Wende Tan,Liucheng Guo,Caifeng Shan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出OPC16K大规模基准数据集（含16,245张图像，分为伪装目标、纯背景和非伪装目标三类）以及OPCNet伪装感知网络，将伪装目标检测从纯分割任务重构为定位与存在性推理的联合任务，以解决现有方法在开放世界中产生严重误检的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有伪装目标检测方法大多基于封闭世界假设，即假设每张输入图像都包含伪装目标，忽略了纯背景或非伪装目标的真实场景，导致在开放环境中部署时产生严重的误报。

Method: 提出OPCNet网络，包含三个关键模块：层次化存在性推理用于区分伪装目标、纯背景和非伪装目标三种场景；相似性感知的伪装关系建模用于捕捉前景-背景伪装线索；存在性感知的特征细化利用存在性预测来调节分割特征。

Result: 在OPC16K基准上的大量实验表明，OPCNet在提出的真实伪装目标检测评估协议下取得了优越性能，在阴性样本上显著降低了误报率，同时保持了准确的伪装目标分割精度。

Conclusion: 通过将伪装目标检测从纯分割任务重新定义为目标定位与伪装存在性推理的联合问题，并配合大规模真实场景基准数据集，有效解决了开放世界中伪装目标检测的误检问题。

Abstract: Camouflaged object detection (COD) aims to segment objects that are visually concealed in their surroundings and has attracted increasing attention in recent years. However, most existing COD methods are developed under a closed-world assumption, where each input image is assumed to contain a camouflaged object. This assumption ignores realistic scenarios with pure backgrounds or non-camouflaged objects, causing existing models to produce severe false positives when deployed in open-world environments. To address this limitation, we propose OPC16K, a large-scale benchmark for realistic COD. OPC16K contains 16,245 images from 14 sources and is carefully organized into camouflaged-object images, pure background images, and non-camouflaged-object images, enabling comprehensive evaluation of both segmentation quality and negative-sample rejection. Based on this benchmark, we further propose OPCNet, a presence-aware camouflage network that reformulates COD from a pure segmentation task into a joint problem of object localization and camouflage existence reasoning. Specifically, OPCNet introduces hierarchical existence reasoning to distinguish CO, BG, and NOCOD scenarios, similarity-aware camouflage relation modeling to capture foreground-background camouflage cues, and existence-aware feature refinement to regulate segmentation features with existence predictions. Extensive experiments on OPC16K demonstrate that OPCNet achieves superior performance under the proposed realistic COD evaluation protocol, significantly reducing false positives on negative samples while maintaining accurate camouflaged-object segmentation. Code and dataset will be released at https://github.com/2231122/OPCOD.

</details>


### [112] [SAR2Agri: Learning SAR Intensity Representations for Agricultural Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11142)
*Moti Rattan Gupta,Anupam Sobti*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个仅使用SAR强度影像进行自监督学习的农业监测预训练流程，通过改进时间预文本任务（掩码和课程学习）来捕捉物候特征，在SICKLE基准上实现84.9%的作物类型映射IoU，超越光学基线和现有SAR基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 农业监测面临复杂的时间和物候动态挑战，虽然SAR卫星提供全天候成像能力，但现有多模态基础模型依赖光学影像对齐，SAR专用基础模型又主要聚焦于目标检测、洪水制图和土地覆盖分类，缺乏专门针对农业应用的自监督预训练方案。

Method: 提出仅使用SAR强度影像的自监督学习流程，通过掩码和课程学习改进物候启发的时间预文本任务，增强预训练流程捕获SAR物候特征的能力。

Result: 在SICKLE基准上，最终模型在作物类型映射任务上达到84.9% IoU，比光学基线高出15.3个百分点，比现有SAR基线高出2.2个百分点。

Conclusion: 实验结果表明所提出的预训练流程在农业监测任务上对SAR强度编码器具有显著有效性，为农业应用提供了不依赖光学影像的纯SAR预训练方案。

Abstract: Agricultural monitoring faces unique challenges, arising from the landscape's complex temporal, phenological, and climate dynamics, yet monitoring them is critical for ensuring food security. Synthetic Aperture Radar (SAR) satellites offer all-weather day-night imaging capability supporting key monitoring tasks including crop type mapping, yield prediction and phenological event detection. Existing multimodal remote sensing foundation models including TerraMind and CopernicusFM learn SAR representations by grounding them in optical imagery using joint encoding and contrastive learning techniques, while SAR-specific foundation models such as SAR-JEPA, SARMAE, and SAR-W-MixMAE primarily focus on target detection, flood mapping, and land cover classification applications. Recent work has introduced phenology inspired temporal pretext tasks with optical imagery which has shown strong performance on agricultural downstream tasks. In this work, we propose the first self-supervised learning pipeline focused on using only SAR intensity imagery for agricultural applications. We improve the temporal pretext tasks through masking and curriculum learning to enhance the pretraining pipeline's ability to capture phenological features from SAR. On the SICKLE benchmark, our final model achieves 84.9% IoU on crop type mapping, outperforming optical baselines (by 15.3 pt) and existing SAR baselines (by 2.2 pt), demonstrating the effectiveness of our proposed pipeline for pretraining SAR intensity encoders for agricultural monitoring.

</details>


### [113] [PRMU: A Corpus-Free Benchmark for Person-Centric Knowledge Unlearning in Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11149)
*Huafeng Chen,Yueming Lyu,Ziyuan Chen,Wenda Tan,Chenyang Si,Liucheng Guo,Caifeng Shan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出PRMU基准，用于评估多模态大语言模型在无原始语料条件下的现实人物知识删除场景中的机器遗忘表现，并引入SGPE轻量级基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLM机器遗忘方法通常假设可以访问原始遗忘和保留语料，但这在现实删除场景中往往不可用。为此需要一种无需原始语料的多模态遗忘评估基准。

Method: 构建PRMU基准，聚焦自然获取的人物相关知识，通过多样化的文本和视觉探针（含对抗评估和细粒度局部性分析）评估模型能否删除目标知识同时保留相关知识。同时提出SGPE基线方法，包括知识位移、受保护参数空间编辑和局部性感知多模态控制。

Result: 实验表明现有遗忘方法常面临遗忘-局部性不利权衡，在激进遗忘设置下局部性显著下降，且易受多模态知识重新激活影响。SGPE在目标遗忘、局部性保持和通用多模态效用之间提供了有竞争力的权衡。

Conclusion: PRMU可促进面向现实可扩展多模态机器遗忘的研究，SGPE为无语料遗忘提供了一个有效基线，代码和数据集将开源。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have demonstrated remarkable capabilities in storing and recalling rich person-related knowledge, raising increasing concerns about reliable knowledge removal. However, existing machine unlearning approaches for MLLMs typically assume access to original forget and retain corpora, which are often unavailable in realistic deletion scenarios. To address this limitation, we introduce PRMU, a benchmark for evaluating corpus-free multimodal unlearning under realistic person-centric deletion requests. PRMU focuses on naturally acquired person-related knowledge and evaluates whether models can remove target knowledge while preserving related knowledge through diverse textual and visual probes, including adversarial evaluation and fine-grained locality analysis. To facilitate research in this setting, we further introduce Similarity-Gated Projection Editing (SGPE), a lightweight corpus-free unlearning baseline with knowledge displacement, protected parameter-space editing, and locality-aware multimodal control. Extensive experiments on representative MLLMs reveal that existing unlearning methods often suffer from unfavorable forgetting-locality trade-offs, with significant locality degradation under aggressive forgetting settings, and remain vulnerable to multimodal knowledge reactivation. Meanwhile, SGPE provides a competitive trade-off between target forgetting, locality preservation, and general multimodal utility. We hope PRMU can facilitate future research toward realistic and scalable multimodal machine unlearning. Code and dataset will be released at https://github.com/2231122/PRMU.

</details>


### [114] [CausalSplat: Towards Comprehensive Hierarchical Reasoning in 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11150)
*Jiayu Ding,Meilu Song,Yun Chen,Wei Gao,Ge Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出推理3D高斯分割任务及两个基准（Causal-LERF和Causal-ScanNet），并提出了CausalSplat框架，通过将视觉语言模型与3D场景图结合来解耦显式结构感知与隐式逻辑推理，在推理基准上达到SOTA并展现出良好的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3DGS开放词汇场景理解方法局限于显式查询，无法理解隐式意图、复杂空间约束和常识推理等实际具身交互所需的能力，存在显著差距。

Method: 构建了Causal-LERF和Causal-ScanNet两个推理基准，系统评估常识、空间、affordance和反事实推理；提出CausalSplat框架，将视觉语言模型与3D场景图整合，解耦显式结构感知与隐式逻辑推理。

Result: 现有SOTA方法在推理基准上表现不佳，而CausalSplat在推理基准上达到SOTA性能，并在标准的引用和开放词汇3D分割任务上表现出强大的泛化能力。

Conclusion: CausalSplat有效弥合了3DGS分割中推理能力的缺口，为具身智能场景下的3D场景理解提供了新范式。

Abstract: While 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) has advanced open vocabulary scene understanding, existing methods remain confined to explicit queries. They struggle to interpret implicit intents, complex spatial constraints, and commonsense reasoning required for practical embodied interactions. To address this gap, we introduce the task of reasoning 3D Gaussian segmentation and construct two benchmarks, Causal-LERF and Causal-ScanNet. These benchmarks systematically evaluate commonsense, spatial, affordance, and counterfactual reasoning. Evaluations reveal that current state of the art methods perform poorly on these reasoning challenges. Therefore, we propose CausalSplat, a framework that integrates vision-language models with 3D scene graphs to disentangle explicit structural perception from implicit logical inference. Extensive experiments demonstrate that CausalSplat achieves state of the art performance on our reasoning benchmarks while showing strong generalizability on standard referring and open vocabulary 3D segmentation tasks. Project Page: https://jiayuding031020.github.io/CausalSplat

</details>


### [115] [VidForensics-M1: Meta-Detection Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Temporal Grounding for AI-Generated Video Forensics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11201)
*Bowei Liu,Zheng Lu,Yuhan Bian,Xinchen Zhang,Xingming Shui,Yuesheng Huang,Xuhuan Li,Zihao Liu,Yifan Yang,Jun Zhou,Xiu Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文首次将元检测（meta-detection）引入AI生成视频检测领域，提出VidForensics-M1模型，通过证据引导的奖励再分配机制，在强化学习框架中联合优化预测标签和时间定位证据，实现了对未知场景和新生成器的鲁棒泛化检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于MLLM的视频伪造检测器主要依赖监督微调或标签级强化学习，粗粒度的监督信号限制了模型对未见场景和新涌现视频生成器的泛化能力，需要更可靠的证据信号和更有效的优化机制。

Method: 提出证据引导的奖励再分配（Evidence-Guided Reward Redistribution）机制，在强化学习中对标签正确的响应按证据质量重新分配奖励；同时构建自动化数据管道，通过边界帧条件视频生成模型替换时间片段来生成配对的真假视频对，提供可验证的时间定位证据。

Result: 大量实验表明VidForensics-M1有效利用可验证的时间证据，实现了鲁棒且可泛化的AI生成视频检测能力。

Conclusion: 时间定位比文本推理提供更客观可验证的伪造证据，将该类证据融入强化学习可显著提升检测器的细粒度伪造定位能力与泛化性能。

Abstract: Recent advances in video generation models have significantly improved the realism of synthetic videos, blurring the boundary between generated and authentic content and raising concerns about misinformation. Existing MLLM-based detectors mainly rely on supervised fine-tuning or label-level reinforcement learning, where coarse supervision limits generalization to unseen scenarios and emerging video generators. To overcome these limitations, we are the first to introduce \textbf{meta-detection} into AI-generated video detection, enabling reliable forgery detection by jointly optimizing predicted labels and supporting evidence within reinforcement learning. This paradigm requires reliable evidence signals and effective mechanisms to integrate them into label-level optimization. Textual rationales provide semantic descriptions of forgery artifacts, but their generation and verification depend on external models, making supervision vulnerable to hallucinations and semantic biases. In contrast, temporal grounding provides more objective and verifiable evidence, as manipulated intervals can be precisely controlled during forgery construction. Based on this insight, we propose an automated data construction pipeline that generates paired real-fake videos by replacing temporal segments with boundary-frame-conditioned video generation models. Furthermore, we introduce \textbf{Evidence-Guided Reward Redistribution}, which performs evidence-aware credit assignment by redistributing rewards among label-correct responses according to evidence quality. This preserves reliable label supervision while encouraging detectors to acquire fine-grained and verifiable forgery localization capabilities. Extensive experiments demonstrate that \textbf{VidForensics-M1} effectively leverages verifiable temporal evidence to achieve robust and generalizable AI-generated video detection.

</details>


### [116] [Capturing Uncertainty in Human Motion for Representation Learning in Soccer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11203)
*Yizhou Xu,Lars Bretzner,Tiesheng Wang,Atsuto Maki*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于未来运动预测的自监督表征学习框架，用于理解足球场景中的3D骨骼人体运动。通过引入条件模块对离散化未来运动建模概率分布，显式学习运动的多模态性，在大型足球运动员追踪数据上显著提升运动预测精度，并能良好迁移到多项足球下游任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人体运动天然具有不确定性，仅预测单一未来轨迹无法充分捕捉运动动态和有效表征。为了学习更具表达力的运动表征，需要对多种可能的未来运动进行建模，而非依赖单一确定性预测。

Method: 以未来运动预测作为自监督学习目标；引入一个运动预测的条件模块，在3D欧几里得空间中对离散化未来运动建模概率分布，并通过未来轨迹提供显式监督来学习多模态分布。

Result: 在大型足球运动员追踪数据集上，该方法显著提升了运动预测准确率；学到的表征可有效迁移至多项足球下游任务，展现出强大的跨任务泛化能力。

Conclusion: 通过显式建模多模态未来运动分布，自监督预测任务能够学习到高质量、可迁移的人体运动表征，验证了该框架在足球场景下的有效性和通用性。

Abstract: This paper presents a self-supervised representation learning framework for understanding 3D skeleton-based human motion in soccer, using future motion prediction as the learning objective. Since human motion is inherently uncertain, accounting for multiple plausible futures is essential for capturing the underlying motion dynamics and learning effective representations. To this end, we introduce a conditioning module for motion prediction that models a probabilistic distribution over discretized future motions in 3D Euclidean space, learning multimodality with explicit supervision from future trajectories. Experiments on large-scale soccer player tracking data show that our approach substantially improves motion prediction accuracy. Moreover, the learned representations effectively transfer to multiple soccer downstream applications, demonstrating strong cross-task generalization.

</details>


### [117] [AdvFD: Boosting Visual Generation via Adversarial Fr'echet Distance Loss](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11205)
*Mingju Gao,Jingkai Zhou,Kun Gai,Changqian Yu,Hao Tang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出对抗Fréchet距离（AdvFD），通过引入对抗学习的可学习表征来补充现有Fréchet损失中静态预训练特征空间的不足，从而解决Fréchet hacking问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的Fréchet损失依赖静态预训练特征空间，这些特征空间提供了不完整且固定的真实与生成分布差异视图，导致直接优化Fréchet目标时出现Fréchet hacking——目标度量持续提升但视觉质量和在其他特征空间的Fréchet对齐停滞或恶化。

Method: AdvFD在FD-Loss的静态表征目标基础上，引入一个对抗学习的可学习表征：该表征对抗性地最大化真实与生成样本之间的Fréchet差异，而生成器在自适应特征空间中最小化相同差异。为防止对抗表征通过特征放大平凡地增加目标，引入真实特征白化（real-feature whitening）来归一化其尺度和协方差几何，稳定min-max优化过程。

Result: 大量实验表明，AdvFD在JiT和pMF两个骨干网络上以及不同模型规模下，均能一致地改进单步生成器后训练效果。

Conclusion: 通过补充对抗性学习的自适应特征空间，AdvFD有效缓解了Fréchet hacking问题，并提升了生成器后训练的整体质量与对齐效果。

Abstract: Fréchet distance has recently emerged as an effective distribution-level objective for generator post-training, complementing the conventional sample-level diffusion and flow-matching losses. However, directly optimizing Fréchet objectives can cause Fréchet hacking. The target metrics keep improving, but visual quality and Fréchet alignment in other feature spaces may stagnate or deteriorate. We attribute this failure to the static pretrained feature spaces used by existing Fréchet losses. These feature spaces provide incomplete and fixed views of the differences between real and generated distributions. To address this limitation, we propose Adversarial Fréchet Distance (AdvFD), which complements the static representation targets in FD-Loss with a calibrated adversarially learned representation. AdvFD augments the original static Fréchet objective with a learnable representation that adversarially maximizes the Fréchet discrepancy between real and generated samples, while the generator minimizes the same discrepancy in the resulting adaptive feature space. To prevent the adversarial representation from trivially increasing the objective through feature amplification, we further introduce real-feature whitening, which normalizes its scale and covariance geometry and stabilizes the min--max optimization. Extensive experiments show that AdvFD consistently improves one-step generator post-training across both JiT and pMF backbones and across different model scales.

</details>


<div id='cs.CL'></div>

# cs.CL [[Back]](#toc)

### [118] [LLM Agents Factory: Retrieval of Domain-Specific LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09934)
*Vitalii Belov,Artyom Sosedka,Andrey Sakhovskiy,Elizaveta Kovtun,Artyom Boyarskikh,Semen Budennyy*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出LLM Agents Factory，一种基于检索的框架，通过超过2万个预定义的智能体配置文件，按需构造领域特定且基于Wikipedia的智能体，以低成本高效率的方式替代动态智能体生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体虽能通过角色分解提升任务性能，但实时为每个用户请求动态设计智能体存在计算成本高和不稳定性问题，限制了实际工业部署。

Method: 构建包含超过2万个预定义智能体配置文件的数据库，支持两种模式：(1)通过语义搜索检索智能体配置文件；(2)将检索结果蒸馏为紧凑的微调模型，实现直接生成智能体。

Result: 在MMLU、BIG-bench和BIG-bench Hard的单智能体场景中，基于检索的智能体构建在准确率上超越非智能体基线，同时以显著更低的推理成本匹配使用120B主干模型的AutoGen生成质量。

Conclusion: 从结构化智能体仓库中进行检索，为动态智能体生成提供了一种成本高效、准确且可控的替代方案，能够满足工业应用对严格性能的要求。代码和智能体库已公开。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents improve task performance by decomposing problems into role-specialized behaviors. However, their practical deployment is often limited by the computational cost and instability associated with the on-the-fly agent design for each user request. To address this, we present LLM Agents Factory, a retrieval-based framework that constructs domain-specific and Wikipedia-grounded agents on demand using a base of over 20K predetermined agent profiles. Our framework supports two modes: (1) agent profile retrieval via semantic search and (2) distillation into a compact model fine-tuned for direct agent generation. Experiments on MMLU, BIG-bench, and BIG-bench Hard in a single-agent scenario demonstrate that our retrieval-based agent construction surpasses non-agent baselines in accuracy while matching AutoGen generation quality with a 120B backbone at a substantially lower inference cost. Our work reveals that retrieval from a structured agent repository provides a cost-efficient, accurate, and controllable alternative to dynamic agent generation, responding to the strict demands of industrial applications. We provide the implementation code and the agent base in https://huggingface.co/frontier-ai/llm-agent-factory.

</details>


### [119] [Conflict or Strategy? Asymmetric Role Framing of La France insoumise and Rassemblement National in French News Headlines, 2022-2025](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09936)
*Amr Sobhy*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究分析了2022-2025年间25家法语媒体发布的28,592条关于"不屈法国"（LFI）和"国民联盟"（RN）的新闻标题，发现两者在新闻框架上呈现角色不对称而非价值不对称：LFI更多出现在冲突性框架中，而RN更多出现在战略选举框架中。研究通过三模型LLM注释流水线并结合人类审计验证了这一发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有计算性框架研究多聚焦于价值层面的测量（如正面/负面），忽略了新闻框架中更深层的结构性差异。作者希望探究法国左翼与右翼民粹主义挑战者在新闻标题中是否被对称地框架化为"极端分子"，还是呈现出根本性的不同政治角色，从而填补政治传播研究中对角色分配（role assignment）关注的不足。

Method: 收集2022-2025年间25家法语媒体的28,592条关于LFI和RN的新闻标题，采用三模型LLM注释流水线进行分类，并通过分层人工审计进行验证。运用自助抽样（bootstrapping）和置换检验（permutation tests）检验结果的方向稳定性，同时按媒体类型和时间段进行持久性分析。

Result: 核心发现是角色不对称而非价值不对称：冲突框架和战略博弈框架在不同模型和时间段上更为稳健，而取消合法性（delegitimization）框架较弱。LFI在标题中更多以冲突性修辞出现，RN更多以战略选举修辞出现。该角色差异在所有三个注释模型中方向一致，经过自助抽样和置换检验后仍然成立，并跨媒体家族和大多数时间段保持。次要的道德核算层面（谁被归咎、合法化或被塑造为受害者）主要由媒体机构而非党派决定，聚合层面呈现零效应但掩盖了语料库中最极化的模式。方法论上，注释流水线呈现两层级可靠性特征：冲突和战略博弈框架可靠性最高，角色分配方向稳定但可靠性较低故作为佐证，规范判断构念（合法性、归咎）最弱。

Conclusion: 该论文将政治角色分配作为计算性框架研究的新目标，主张其能分解价值测量所混淆的内容，并建立了构念分层可靠性框架，用于校准政治文本任务中多数投票LLM注释流水线。研究揭示了法国左右民粹主义政党在媒体框架中的结构性差异，但同时也指出道德问责层面的极化模式被媒体归属所掩盖，提醒研究者注意聚合分析的局限性。

Abstract: Do French news headlines frame left- and right-populist challengers as symmetric ``extremes,'' or as fundamentally different political adversaries? We examine 28,592 headlines about La France insoumise (LFI) and Rassemblement National (RN) published by 25 French-language outlets between 2022 and 2025, annotated through a three-model LLM pipeline validated against a stratified human audit. The clearest finding is role asymmetry rather than valence asymmetry: conflict framing and strategic-game framing are more robust across models and time than delegitimization, with AGGRESSOR serving as corroborating role syntax. LFI appears in headlines more often through a conflict register and RN through a strategic-electoral register. This role gap is direction-stable across all three annotation models, survives bootstrapping and permutation tests, and persists across outlet families and most of 2022-2025. A secondary moral-accounting layer (who is blamed, legitimized, or cast as a victim) is structured by outlet rather than party, producing aggregate nulls that conceal some of the corpus's most polarized patterns. Methodologically, the annotation pipeline reveals a two-tier reliability profile: conflict and strategic-game framing achieve the strongest human validation and cross-model stability; actor role is direction-stable but treated as corroborating because its audit reliability is lower; normative-judgment constructs (legitimacy, blame) are weaker. The paper contributes political-role assignment as a target for computational framing research that decomposes what valence-based measures conflate, and establishes a construct-stratified reliability framework for calibrating majority-vote LLM annotation pipelines in political text tasks.

</details>


### [120] [Carefully Considering Culture: Analyzing LLM Alignment in Single- and Multi-Cultural Settings using Cultural Consensus Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09937)
*Krishna Pothugunta,John P. Lalor*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文利用文化共识理论（CCT）建模大型语言模型对多国文化规范理解的细微差异，发现模型常无法形成内聚共识或过度规则化共识，并通过显式建模组内方差提供诊断工具，以区分模型反映真实人类多样性还是算法同质化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有NLP研究主要基于分布模式探测语言模型对各国文化规范的理解，忽视了群体共识或一国之内可能存在的多元文化环境。作者希望弥补这一不足，引入文化人类学的文化共识理论来建模这种多维度细微差别。

Method: 将文化共识理论（CCT）应用于世界价值观调查（WVS）数据，覆盖10个国家和12个领域，通过显式表示组内方差来建模文化结构，并提供可操作的诊断框架。

Result: 研究发现模型在模拟文化结构时经常出现问题：要么无法形成内聚的共识，要么过度规则化共识，导致对真实人类文化多样性的错误表征。

Conclusion: CCT框架能够提供有价值的诊断信号，帮助评估语言模型是真实反映人类文化多样性还是陷入算法同质化的倾向。

Abstract: Recent work in NLP has probed large language models for their understanding of cultural norms across countries. However, this work typically considers distributional patterns, ignoring group consensus or possible multicultural environments within a country. In this work, we leverage cultural consensus theory (CCT) from cultural anthropology to model such multidimensional nuance. Applying CCT to the World Values Survey (WVS) across 10 countries and 12 domains, we demonstrate that models frequently misrepresent cultural structures by either failing to form cohesive consensus or severely over-regularizing consensus. Through explicit representation of intra-group variance, CCT provides actionable diagnostics to evaluate when models reflect true human diversity versus algorithmic homogenization.

</details>


### [121] [The Multilingual Quantization Tax: Structural Collapse and Typological Fragility in Edge SLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09941)
*Mohammad Wathiq Soualhi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个零样本多语言基准，评估4位权重量化在Gemma 4和Qwen 3.5等小型语言模型上的性能退化（量化税），揭示了参数截断暴露出的预训练不平等现象，并识别出四种量化相关现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对4位权重量化性能退化的评估主要局限于英语，缺乏对低资源语言和非拉丁文字系统的多语言评估，无法全面理解量化对语言多样性场景的影响。

Method: 在Gemma 4和Qwen 3.5架构上，使用MMLU ProX Lite和GlobalPIQA基准，对八种类型学上多样的语言进行零样本多语言4位量化评估。

Result: 识别出四种现象：（1）类型学脆弱性：低资源和非拉丁文字在特定架构上出现表征崩溃；（2）母语脆弱性悖论：基础预训练路径对精度损失保护有限；（3）领域特定遗忘：跨语言多步路由退化而联想型软科学记忆保持稳健；（4）量化抵抗：高度饱和且类型学对齐的领域抵抗确定性退化，量化后性能提升受统计噪声限制。

Conclusion: 4位量化并非对所有语言和领域都有相同的性能代价，其影响高度依赖语言资源的丰富程度、文字类型和领域特性，需要更细致的多语言量化评估框架。

Abstract: While 4-bit weight quantization is critical for deploying Small Language Models (SLMs) on edge devices, evaluations of the resulting performance degradation-the quantization tax-remain overwhelmingly English-centric. We present a zero-shot multilingual evaluation of 4-bit quantization across the Gemma 4 and Qwen 3.5 architectures. Evaluating on eight typo-logically diverse languages using MMLU ProX Lite and GlobalPIQA, we show parameter truncation exposes deep pre-training inequalities. We identify four phenomena: (1) Typological Fragility: low-resource and specific non-Latin scripts suffer representational collapse via architecture-specific double dissociations, failing to generate valid task logits; (2) Home Language Fragility Paradox: foundational pre-training pathways provide limited precision loss protection; (3) Domain-Specific Forgetting: multi-step cross-lingual routing degrades while associative soft-science recall remains robust; and (4) Quantization Resistance: highly saturated, typologically aligned domains resist deterministic degradation, with post-quantization performance gains bounded by statistical noise.

</details>


### [122] [When Chain-of-Thought Helps and When It Hurts: An Empirical Investigation of the Serial-Depth Bottleneck in LLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09942)
*Tughanbulut Kurtulush*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文挑战了"链式思维(CoT)提示普适提升LLM推理能力"的假设，通过H_dp带宽界框架论证CoT本质上是"带宽旁路"——帮助超出Transformer单次前向容量限制的串行计算，而对已适配的任务则冗余。实验跨3个模型、5个基准，发现高串行深度任务(GSM8K、MATH)上CoT带来+54至+68个百分点恢复差距，浅层任务(MMLU、ARC)上CoT结构性冗余，中间类任务(HumanEval)呈现模型规模依赖的转变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主流假设认为CoT提示可以普遍提升LLM推理能力，但缺乏系统性验证。论文希望通过概念性框架与实证分析，检验CoT是否在所有任务中都有增益，并探究其作用机制是否与任务的串行计算深度相关。

Method: 基于H_dp带宽界(Chen et al., 2024)提出"串行深度梯度"假设：单次前向(无CoT)准确率随每项串行深度单调下降，而CoT近似深度不变。在3个指令微调模型(Qwen-2.5-7B/32B、Llama-3.1-8B)和5个标准NLP基准上，在实用上下文长度下测量CoT效果，并使用Spearman相关和McNemar检验进行统计分析，且预先在OSF注册。

Result: 高深度P-complete任务上CoT恢复差距为+54至+68个百分点；浅层TC^0任务上CoT无显著效果(Delta在0.0至+4.6个百分点)；中间类L任务(HumanEval)呈模型规模依赖转变(+23.2pp/32B、+9.1pp/8B、-28.7pp/7B)。跨基准深度-恢复相关性Spearman rho=0.661(p=0.007)，15个基准级McNemar检验中9个在Bonferroni校正后显著。

Conclusion: CoT并非通用的推理增强器，而是作为带宽旁路发挥作用：它有助于超出单次前向容量限制的串行计算，对已适配的任务则冗余。这一发现对CoT在各类任务中的使用策略具有重要指导意义。

Abstract: It is widely assumed that chain-of-thought (CoT) prompting universally improves LLM reasoning. We investigate this through the conceptual framework of the H_dp bandwidth bound (Chen et al., 2024): although the formal bound binds only asymptotically (at astronomically large prompt lengths), it identifies a real architectural bottleneck -- serial computation exceeding a transformer's single-pass capacity must be externalised, which is what CoT does. Our central finding is a within-benchmark serial-depth gradient: single-pass (no-CoT) accuracy degrades monotonically with per-item serial depth, while CoT is approximately depth-invariant. We measure CoT effects across three instruction-tuned models (Qwen-2.5-7B/32B, Llama-3.1-8B) and five standard NLP benchmarks at practical context lengths. On high-depth P-complete tasks (GSM8K, MATH), CoT gives a +54 to +68 pp recovery gap across all models. On shallow TC^0 tasks (MMLU, ARC), CoT is structurally redundant (Delta in [0.0, +4.6] pp, no significant negative effect) -- though high no-CoT baselines (up to 95% on ARC) may reflect contamination, so this null is not a clean architectural test. The intermediate class L (HumanEval) shows a model-size-dependent transition: +23.2 pp (32B), +9.1 pp (8B), -28.7 pp (7B). The cross-benchmark depth-recovery correlation is Spearman rho = 0.661 (p = 0.007, n = 15); 9 of 15 benchmark-level McNemar tests are significant after Bonferroni correction. Pre-registered on OSF, our results indicate that CoT is not a universal reasoning enhancer but acts as a bandwidth bypass: it helps serial computation that strains single-pass capacity and is redundant for tasks that already fit.

</details>


### [123] [Position Encoding in Transformers: From Absolute and Relative Methods to Rotary Position Embeddings and Long-Context Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10021)
*Jiguo Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文是一篇关于Transformer位置编码的技术综述，系统梳理了从绝对位置嵌入（正弦、可学习）到相对位置编码（Shaw、Transformer-XL、T5、ALiBi、RoPE）的演进脉络，并深入分析了多种长上下文扩展方法（如位置插值、NTK缩放、YaRN、LongRoPE等），强调位置编码能力的提升不等于长上下文泛化能力的提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Self-attention本身无法编码token顺序，位置编码是Transformer架构的关键组成部分。然而现有位置编码方法种类繁多、技术路线各异，缺乏统一的比较框架与分析视角，且长上下文扩展方法层出不穷，亟需系统性梳理。

Method: 采用统一分析框架对比各类位置编码方法，从注入位置（位置表示 vs 注意力分数）、计算成本、KV缓存兼容性、长度外推能力四个维度展开；并从频率分配、注意力重缩放、训练长度、目标上下文长度等角度剖析长上下文扩展技术，同时总结实现细节与评估协议。

Result: 推导了RoPE如何将绝对位置索引转换为Query-Key内积中的相对相位差；系统比较了六类主流位置编码方法的优缺点；分析了八种长上下文扩展技术的核心设计差异；总结了代表性大语言模型中的位置编码选型。

Conclusion: 核心结论是：位置编码在训练长度之外计算位置特征的能力并不意味着可靠的长上下文泛化。上下文扩展必须通过短上下文保留、逐位置困惑度、检索、推理和长上下文代码任务等多维度评估来验证。

Abstract: Self-attention models content-dependent interactions between tokens but does not by itself encode token order. Position encoding addresses this limitation by introducing absolute coordinates, relative distances, or position-dependent rotations into Transformer representations and attention scores. This technical survey develops a unified account of sinusoidal and learned absolute position embeddings, Shaw-style relative position representations, Transformer-XL, T5 relative position bias, ALiBi, and Rotary Position Embeddings (RoPE). We derive how RoPE converts absolute position indices into relative phase differences in Query-Key inner products and compare these methods in terms of where position is injected, computational cost, compatibility with KV caching, and length extrapolation. We then examine long-context extensions, including Position Interpolation, RoPE scaling laws, NTK-aware scaling, Dynamic NTK, NTK-by-parts, YaRN, LongRoPE, and LongRoPE2, with emphasis on frequency allocation, attention rescaling, training length, and target context length. We also summarize implementation considerations, evaluation protocols, and position-encoding choices in representative large language models. A central conclusion is that the ability to compute positional features beyond the training length does not imply reliable long-context generalization; context extension must be evaluated through short-context retention, position-wise perplexity, retrieval, reasoning, and long-context code tasks.

</details>


### [124] [PERCEPT: A Corpus for POS Tagging and Analysis of Persian-English Code-Mixing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10109)
*Ghazal Kalhor,Zahra Jafari,Amirarsalan Shahbazi,Behnam Bahrak*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了PERCEPT，这是首个公开可用的大规模波斯语-英语代码混合语料库，包含6,800条来自X、Instagram和Digikala的帖子，并使用Universal Dependencies POS标签对代码混合词进行标注。研究还提出了一个LLM辅助的标注框架，并在此基础上进行了首个跨多平台波斯语-英语代码混合的综合语言学分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管代码混合语料库已为多种语言对开发，但波斯语-英语代码混合研究相对不足。现有的波斯语资源缺乏针对代码混合词的Universal Dependencies POS标注，这限制了对该语言现象的语言学分析以及语法感知NLP模型的开发。

Method: 研究者构建了PERCEPT语料库，收集了6,800条来自X、Instagram和Digikala三个平台的帖子。提出了一种LLM辅助的标注框架，能够自动分配POS标签和文档级主题，并通过人工评估验证了自动标注与黄金标注之间的一致性。

Result: 研究发现名词是代码混合词中最主要的词类类别，而其他POS类别的分布在各个平台上有所不同。此外，代码混合词的位置分布在各平台间高度一致，而触发效应在Digikala平台上更为显著。

Conclusion: PERCEPT填补了波斯语-英语代码混合语料库的空白，为未来语言学研究和语法感知NLP模型开发提供了宝贵资源，同时揭示了跨平台代码混合行为的共性与差异。

Abstract: Social media has become a major venue for multilingual communication, where users frequently mix multiple languages within a single utterance. Although code-mixed corpora have been developed for several language pairs, Persian-English code-mixing remains relatively underexplored. Existing Persian resources lack Universal Dependencies (UD) part-of-speech (POS) annotations for code-mixed words, limiting both linguistic analyses and the development of syntax-aware NLP models. To address this gap, we introduce PERCEPT, the first publicly available large-scale Persian-English code-mixed corpus annotated with Universal Dependencies POS tags for code-mixed words. The dataset comprises 6,800 posts collected from X, Instagram, and Digikala. We further present an LLM-assisted annotation framework that automatically assigns POS tags and document-level topics. Human evaluation demonstrates high agreement between the automatically generated annotations and gold annotations, confirming the reliability of the annotations. Using PERCEPT, we conduct the first comprehensive linguistic analysis of Persian-English code-mixing across multiple social media platforms. Our analyses reveal that nouns are the predominant category for code-mixed words, while the distributions of other POS categories vary across platforms. We further find that the positional distribution of code-mixed words is remarkably consistent across platforms, whereas the triggering effect is substantially more pronounced in Digikala. PERCEPT is publicly available at https://github.com/kalhorghazal/PERCEPT.

</details>


### [125] [The Parser Already Knows: Lightweight Bias Correction in Constrained Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10137)
*Işıl Özgü,Yaoxuan Wu,Guy Van den Broeck,Miryung Kim*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种轻量级、离线训练的logit校正方法，利用增量解析过程中已有的解析器和词法分析器状态，恢复被语法约束解码（GCD）遮蔽所扭曲的语言模型概率分布，在不增加推理开销的前提下兼顾语法合规性和输出质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GCD通过硬性遮蔽保证语法有效性，但会扭曲模型的原始概率分布，导致生成结果偏向"合法但次优"的输出；在线采样虽能恢复分布但计算开销过大。因此需要在输出质量与推理延迟之间寻求更好的平衡。

Method: 利用增量解析过程中天然维护的解析器和词法分析器内部状态（这些状态在进行遮蔽时已被计算，提取开销可忽略），结合候选下一个token，训练一个轻量级的离线logit校正器，无需改动基础LM的权重。文中还探讨了仅依赖候选token的最轻量变体，利用token本身隐含的lookahead信息。

Result: 在多种语法上，该校正方法显著缩小了遮蔽分布与LM真实分布之间的差距，一致优于纯遮蔽方法和在线采样方法；即使最轻量级变体（仅使用候选token）也能达到或超过两种基线。

Conclusion: 通过恢复遮蔽所移除的概率质量，该方法调和了语言模型的概率完整性与语法合规性之间的矛盾，以极小的额外开销实现了高质量与低延迟的兼得。

Abstract: Grammar Constrained Decoding (GCD) forces Language Models (LMs) to produce syntactically valid outputs by masking out non-conforming tokens at each step. However, rigid masking distorts the model's underlying probability distribution, often biasing generation toward valid but suboptimal outputs. While online sampling restores this distribution, it requires computationally expensive iterative resampling. As a result, existing methods force a compromise between output quality and inference latency. Our key insight is that the internal parser and lexer states inherently maintained during incremental parsing already encode future grammatical validity -- exactly the information required to restore the LM's true distribution. We propose a lightweight, offline-trained logit correction conditioned on this syntactic and lexical state together with candidate next tokens. Because these states are already computed as a necessary part of incremental parsing for masking, extracting them adds negligible overhead while leaving the base LM's weights completely untouched. Across several grammars, this correction substantially closes the gap between the masked distribution and the LM's true distribution, consistently outperforming both masking and online sampling. Even its lightest variant, which relies on the candidate next token alone, still matches or exceeds both baselines: the next token itself carries an implicit lookahead, much like how parsers commonly use a lookahead token to resolve ambiguous decisions. By restoring the probability mass that masking removes, it reconciles the LM's probabilistic integrity with grammar conformance.

</details>


### [126] [Multimodal Item Parameter Estimation using Simulated Response Probabilitie](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10154)
*Christopher Ormerod,YoungKoung Kim*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文利用基于Qwen3.5微调的多模态大语言模型，通过提示和训练使其复现学生在不同能力水平下的选项选择概率，从而隐式捕捉MCM和3PL模型曲线所编码的响应概率，并直接利用模型预测的选项概率在测试集上准确近似题目难度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统IRT模型（如3PL和MCM）在拟合学生响应模式时通常依赖大量参数估计和复杂的统计推断，而大语言模型具备强大的模式学习能力，若能直接学习学生的系统误差模式，则有望简化题目难度的估计过程并提升效率。

Method: 基于Qwen3.5微调一个多模态大语言模型，将包含图像和文本的多选题与标注的学生能力水平作为训练条件，通过提示学习和微调使模型复现不同能力水平下学生的选项选择概率，从而隐式学习3PL和MCM曲线背后的响应概率分布。

Result: 在留出测试集上，模型能够利用预测的选项概率准确近似题目难度，证明LLM隐含地捕捉了3PL和MCM曲线所编码的学生响应概率模式。

Conclusion: 该方法验证了多模态大语言模型可以作为一种有效的工具，从学生响应模式中隐式重建经典IRT模型曲线并估计题目难度，为自动化题目分析提供了新的技术路径。

Abstract: We present results from reconstructing multiple-choice model (MCM) and three-parameter logistic (3PL) model curves using a fine-tuned multimodal large language model (LLM) based on Qwen3.5. The model is prompted and fine-tuned to replicate choice probabilities across a large training corpus of multiple-choice items containing both image and text stimuli, conditioned on a labeled set of student ability levels. By learning to reproduce the systematic error patterns of students across a discrete range of abilities, the LLM implicitly captures the underlying response probabilities encoded in the 3PL and MCM curves. This allows us to accurately approximate item difficulty on a held-out test set directly from the model's predicted option probabilities.

</details>


### [127] [Similarity Gates Approve Reversals: A Validity Audit of Embedding-Cosine Thresholds in Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10216)
*Scott E. Frias*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文对基于嵌入余弦相似度的文本比较质量门（如去重过滤、语义缓存、漂移防护、答案评分器等）进行了系统审计，发现这类门控在语义保持判断上存在系统性失效：它们测量的是措辞变化而非语义变化，导致安全门反向触发（如"停药"与"给药"的相反指令相似度高至0.9608）。研究通过90个配置-阈值-任务单元实验证明平衡准确率中位数仅0.525，且评估本身也受同一混杂因素污染。简单的修复方案（编码器替换、重叠条件门、NLI替代）均告失败，但匹配对审计揭示最强配置可达AUROC 0.79-0.90，表明有效仪器可构建但必须采用匹配对设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生产环境中普遍部署的嵌入余弦相似度门控被当作语义等价性的判定工具，但审计发现分数实际反映的是措辞变化程度而非语义保持程度。在门控存在的关键场景（如语义保持检测）中两者往往背道而驰：单个词的反转指令（如"撤回"vs"给予"试验药物）可能仅为编辑距离很小的改动，而语义一致的复述往往重写整个句子。这种测量失效直接导致安全检查反向触发，如生产漂移防护门在56个语义破坏变体中捕获0个，却放行了一个语义完全相反的指令。作者的核心动机是揭示这一广泛使用却被系统性误解的测量工具的缺陷，并证明评估方法本身也受到同样的混淆效应污染。

Method: 论文作为对嵌入余弦相似度门控这一测量仪器的审计研究，方法包括：(1) 对生产环境中的漂移防护门进行实际审计，测试56个语义破坏变体及正常样本；(2) 在90个配置-阈值-任务单元中评估五个已上线操作点的平衡准确率；(3) 构建混杂继承的朴素语料库与平衡2x2设计进行对比评估，揭示评估方向反转现象；(4) 测试三种修复方案：编码器替换、重叠条件门（0.750样本内/0.533留出）、NLI替代；(5) 采用匹配对审计（匹配重叠条件下比较反转与复述），在九种配置中发现最强两种达到AUROC 0.79-0.90，证明混淆因素可通过实验设计加以控制。

Result: 关键发现包括：(1) 生产漂移防护门对56个语义破坏突变捕获率为0，且"撤回试验药"vs"给予试验药"这一相反指令的余弦相似度高达0.9608；(2) 90个配置-阈值-任务单元中平衡准确率中位数仅0.525，最高不超过0.700；(3) 朴素语料库评估在18个配置-任务单元中的13个出现AUROC为0.000的完全反转结果（全部18个单元最高仅0.040），而平衡2x2设计下同一批配置达到0.440-0.815；(4) 简单的修复方案——编码器替换、重叠条件门（0.750样本内/0.533留出）、NLI替代——在独立作者留出数据上均退化为随机水平；(5) 匹配对审计下最强的两种配置在匹配重叠条件下区分反转与复述的AUROC达到0.79-0.90。

Conclusion: 嵌入余弦相似度门控在实际部署中测量的是措辞变化而非语义变化，这在安全关键场景中会导致门控反向触发。评估体系本身也受同一混杂因素污染，朴素语料库评估甚至可能给出完全反转的结论（AUROC为0.000），这意味着此前大量依赖此类评估的结论可能不可靠。虽然简单的修复（编码器替换、启发式门控、NLI替代）均失败，但匹配对审计表明嵌入中仍包含有用的语义判别信号（AUROC 0.79-0.90）。作者认为有效的测量仪器是可以构建的，但必须采用匹配对设计来控制重叠混淆因素。论文发布了语料库构建方法、测试框架和冻结结果，以供社区进一步研究。

Abstract: Agent frameworks ship quality gates that compare text blocks by embedding-cosine similarity and decide at a fixed cutoff. Deduplication filters, semantic caches, drift guards, and answer grader gates deploy to answer the question: "Does this text still mean the same thing?" But the score answers a different question: "How much did the wording change?" We audit this gate class as a measurement instrument. In the cases these gates exist to catch, the two can run in opposite ways. Many times, reversing an instruction is a single word edit, while agreement often rephrases a sentence. The consequence is a safety check that fires backwards. The production drift guard we audited caught 0 of 56 meaning-breaking mutations, and one approved item, "withhold the study drug" -> "administer the study drug", came in at cosine 0.9608. We observed five shipped operating points, and balanced accuracy across 90 configuration-threshold-task cells never exceeded 0.700 (median 0.525). The same confounder also corrupted evaluations. A naively built corpus inherits this confounder and can return an inverted verdict, with a decision AUROC exactly 0.000 in 13 of 18 configuration-task cells (at most 0.040 in all 18) against 0.440-0.815 for the same nine configurations under a balanced 2x2 design. Twice in the effort it captured our own headline claims. Obvious repairs fail: an encoder swap and an overlap-conditioned gate (0.750 in-sample, 0.533 held-out) land at chance on separately authored held-out data, and an NLI drop-in did no better. Embeddings do still bear hope here, as the strongest two of nine configurations separated reversal from paraphrase at matched overlap (AUROC 0.79-0.90), but only a matched-pair audit reveals the deployment regime. We release the corpus method, harness, and frozen results, and contend that scores gated this way measure the wrong thing. We believe a valid instrument is buildable.

</details>


### [128] [Off-Axis, On Purpose: Where a Transformer Computes Concepts and Why it Does So](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10251)
*Mark Oskin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文发现Transformer模型的计算分两个阶段：第一阶段（概念阶段）所有子层将信息写入与读出方向近正交的子空间，该子空间隔离了组合与词汇；第二阶段答案通过加法而非旋转来到读出轴上。将模型强制压到读出轴会削减概念工作空间维度但标准基准无法检测到这种变化。该几何结构可以被强制施加——在阶段边界插入固定旋转即可实现，且旋转的选择不影响结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer的中间状态大多偏离其读出方向（unembedding所读取的轴），这种"离轴"位置通常被视为解释的障碍。作者想证明这种离轴几何结构实际上是功能性的，而非噪声或障碍。

Method: 作者分析了12层模型的激活几何，测量每层注意力与读出方向的夹角；通过对比向读出方向移动注意力值（vs随机旋转）量化其损害；测试了早期退出训练（将每层压到读出轴）对概念工作空间维度的影响；并尝试通过损失函数、阶段边界插入旋转、稀疏旋转等多种方式施加该几何结构。

Result: 注意力值在每层与读出方向呈75-96度角；将其移向读出方向的损害比随机旋转大64-84倍，且损害完全来自跨token混合。早期退出训练在标准基准上匹配基线，但将概念工作空间从25维削减到14维。通过损失函数预设几何是彩票（8个种子中6个崩溃），但在阶段边界插入固定旋转可成功，稀疏旋转收敛于全部9个种子，25次运行中13种不同旋转均达到相同质量。

Conclusion: Transformer的离轴几何不是障碍而是功能性的：它通过将组合（composition）与词汇（vocabulary）隔离来实现高效计算。该几何结构可以被自由选择和施加（旋转选择无关紧要），并且是独立于读出方向的可利用自由度。

Abstract: A transformer's answer lives on one axis: the direction its unembedding reads. Its intermediate states largely do not, and that off-axis position is usually treated as an obstacle to interpretation. We show it is functional. A 12-layer model computes in two phases. Through the first, every sublayer writes into a subspace held near-orthogonal to the read-out, attention 75 to 96 degrees off it at every depth. Moving attention's values onto the read-out is 64 to 84 times more damaging than a matched random rotation, and the damage is entirely in cross-token mixing: the subspace insulates composition from the vocabulary. Beneath it the frame itself turns rigidly with depth. In the second phase the answer arrives on-axis, late, and by addition rather than by turning accumulated content onto the read-out.
  Pressing every layer onto the read-out instead, as training for early exit does, matches the baseline on perplexity, LAMBADA and BLiMP while cutting the concept-phase workspace from about twenty-five effective dimensions to fourteen, a change none of those benchmarks register. The geometry can also be imposed, though not by asking for it. Prescribing it through the loss is a lottery: six of eight seeds collapse, because a model told to null its read-out projection obeys most cheaply by discarding dimensions. Inserting one fixed rotation at the phase boundary lands it instead, at baseline quality. A sparse rotation the surrounding weights can absorb converges on all nine seeds, against five of nine for ordinary training. Which rotation is immaterial: twenty-five runs across thirteen distinct ones reach the same quality, and two baselines from different seeds hold their concepts in near-orthogonal frames while agreeing on their read-outs. That freedom is usable: a basis drawn at random and prescribed before training is adopted across the concept phase, with quality unchanged.

</details>


### [129] [TAF-MED: Multi-Turn Safety Refusal Collapse in LLMs Under Declared Self-Treatment Intent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10258)
*Waleed Jamil,Raphael Schmitt*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出TAF-MED基准，包含500个固定三轮医疗对话场景，评估8个大语言模型在4000个对话中的用药安全边界持续性。结果显示71.6%的对话包含不安全响应，61.4%的初始安全响应在后续轮次中崩溃，表明首轮安全不能代表对话全流程的安全持续性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医疗安全基准未单独检验在用户明确表达自我治疗意图后，模型在后续追问轮次中是否仍能维持用药安全边界。首轮响应的安全性不足以衡量多轮对话场景下的整体安全表现，需要专门的基准来评估对话轨迹全流程的安全持续性。

Method: 构建了由医生审阅的TAF-MED基准（500个固定三轮场景），评估8个LLM共4000个对话。使用基于评分量规的自动化评判器将响应标记为SAFE、LEAKY或UNSAFE，并由两位医生独立标注400个随机对话子集进行验证。评估指标包括不安全指导比例、初始安全后崩溃率以及模型排名稳定性。

Result: 71.6%的对话包含不安全响应；61.4%以严格安全响应开头的对话后续崩溃为不安全；模型崩溃率范围为24.4%至96.2%。28对模型中有4对在初始不安全率与崩溃率之间排名反转。自动化标签与医生仲裁参考的一致率达94.3%（κ=0.895）。

Conclusion: 首轮安全性是对话安全持久性的不完整代理指标，需要在完整对话轨迹上评估医疗安全。TAF-MED将发布到Hugging Face以支持多轮医疗安全的可复现研究。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) increasingly provide conversational health information that may influence treatment decisions, yet existing benchmarks do not isolate whether medication-safety boundaries persist across follow-ups after explicit self-treatment intent. We introduce TAF-MED, a physician-reviewed benchmark of 500 fixed three-turn scenarios, and evaluate eight LLMs across 4,000 conversations. A rubric-based automated judge labelled responses as SAFE, LEAKY, or UNSAFE, and two physicians independently annotated a model-balanced random subset of 400 conversations. We assessed unsafe guidance, collapse after a strictly SAFE initial response, and model-ranking stability. Overall, 71.6% of conversations contained an UNSAFE response, and 61.4% of those beginning with a strictly SAFE response later collapsed to UNSAFE; model-level collapse rates ranged from 24.4% to 96.2%. Four of 28 model pairs reversed order between initial unsafe and collapse rates. Automated labels achieved 94.3% agreement with the adjudicated physician reference ($κ= 0.895$). These findings show that first-turn safety is an incomplete proxy for conversational safety persistence and motivate evaluation across complete dialogue trajectories. We will release TAF-MED on Hugging Face to support reproducible research on multi-turn medical safety.

</details>


### [130] [Locally Deployable Small Language Models for Emergency Department Decision Support: A Systematic Benchmark of Fine-Tuning Strategies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10273)
*Qingfeng Zhang,Yuanxiong Guo,Yanmin Gong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究在急诊科（ED）决策支持场景下，系统评估了八种开源小语言模型（SLM）在三种任务（分诊级别预测、专科转诊推荐、诊断预测）上的表现，对比了零样本提示、前缀微调、LoRA和全参数微调四种策略，并以两个商业闭源模型为基线。结果表明，LoRA微调的开源SLM在分诊级别预测和专科转诊推荐上超越商业基线，且能捕捉商业模型遗漏的高危患者，但在诊断预测方面仍有差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型（LLM）在急诊科决策支持中面临两大挑战：一是将患者数据传输至闭源商业LLM存在隐私风险；二是缺乏对本地可部署的开源小语言模型（SLM）微调策略的系统性评估。作者希望探索既能保护隐私又能保持临床竞争力的本地部署方案。

Method: 作者在MIMIC-IV-ED数据集的2,083个病例上，对八种开源SLM使用四种策略（零样本提示、前缀微调、LoRA、全参数微调）进行基准测试，覆盖三个急诊任务（分诊级别预测、专科转诊推荐、诊断预测），并以Claude Haiku 4.5和Claude Sonnet 4.5作为商业闭源基线，同时使用混淆矩阵分析模型对最高危患者的识别能力。

Result: 1) LoRA微调的开源SLM在分诊级别预测和专科转诊推荐上超过商业闭源基线模型；2) 诊断预测对开源SLM仍具挑战性；3) 混淆矩阵分析显示，微调后的开源SLM能识别商业基线遗漏的最高严重级别患者。

Conclusion: 本地可部署的开源SLM在急诊决策支持中能够达到具有临床竞争力的性能，为在隐私约束下部署LLM用于急诊辅助决策提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Deploying large language models (LLMs) for decision support in emergency departments (EDs) faces two major challenges: privacy risks of transmitting patient data to closed-source commercial LLMs and the lack of systematic evaluation of fine-tuning strategies for locally deployable open-source small language models (SLMs). We benchmarked eight open-source SLMs using zero-shot prompting, prefix tuning, Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA), and full fine-tuning on three ED tasks: triage level prediction, specialist referral recommendation, and diagnosis prediction. Using 2,083 MIMIC-IV-ED cases and Claude Haiku 4.5 and Claude Sonnet 4.5 as baselines, we found that LoRA fine-tuned open-source SLMs outperform commercial baselines on triage level prediction and specialist referral recommendation, while diagnosis prediction remains challenging for open-source SLMs. Confusion matrix analysis further shows that fine-tuned open-source SLMs can detect highest-severity patients missed by the commercial baselines. These results demonstrate that locally deployable SLMs can achieve clinically competitive performance for ED decision support.

</details>


### [131] [Cracks in the Foundation: Seemingly Minor Architectural Choices Impact Long Context Extension](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10296)
*Amanda Bertsch,Luca Soldaini,Matthew R. Gormley,Graham Neubig,Hannaneh Hajishirzi,Kyle Lo,Dirk Groeneveld*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文发现四个看似微小的架构决策（归一化方式、GQA、预训练上下文长度、滑动窗口注意力）对长上下文可扩展性会产生累积性的负面影响，组合三个以上可导致下游性能下降高达47%。通过控制变量消融实验（超过17万GPU小时训练），作者发布了26个可比的7B模型集合OlmPool，并发现多种架构在长上下文扩展性上优于Llama 3，同时识别出可归因于具体架构差异的注意力汇和注意力分布模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管过去认为密集Transformer架构内的微小变化对准确率影响有限，但在长上下文场景下该假设可能不成立。作者希望系统性地验证并量化这些架构选择对长上下文可扩展性的影响。

Method: 在固定数据、分词器和扩展策略的条件下，对归一化、GQA、预训练上下文长度、滑动窗口注意力四个维度进行受控消融实验。经过超过170,000 GPU小时的训练，构建了26个可比的7B模型（OlmPool），每个模型均提供长上下文扩展前后的检查点，并分析注意力汇行为与注意力分布模式。

Result: 四个架构决策单独影响较小，但组合三个以上会使下游性能下降高达47%。这些差异无法通过短上下文损失或验证数据集检测。OlmPool中多种架构在长上下文可扩展性上优于Llama 3。分析中发现特定架构特征与注意力汇行为及注意力分布模式之间存在可归因的联系。

Conclusion: 长上下文能力在很大程度上由架构特征驱动，且可在预训练早期通过应用上下文扩展来检测。架构选择应被视为长上下文模型设计中的关键因素，而非可忽略的细节。

Abstract: One might imagine that architectural variations within the dense transformer paradigm have a limited effect on accuracy. However, we demonstrate that this is not the case in the long context setting. Specifically, we show that a set of four minor architectural decisions --- all made by at least one of the Olmo, Llama, and Qwen dense model families --- have a compoundingly negative effect on long context extensibility. Any one of these choices alone has a minor impact on long context performance, but combining three or more can drop the performance downstream by up to 47%. Furthermore, these differences are not detectable from short-context loss or validation datasets. We show that much of the variation in long context ability across model families is driven by these architectural features and detectable from applying context extension early in pretraining. We demonstrate this with controlled ablations that hold data, tokenizer, and extension recipe fixed while varying normalization, GQA, pretraining context length, and sliding window attention. After over 170,000 GPU hours of training, we release the resulting set of models as OlmPool, a set of 26 comparable 7B models with checkpoints before and after long-context extension. This pool includes several architectures that outperform the Llama 3 architecture on long context extensibility. In an analysis of our ablation models, we identify patterns in attention sink behavior and attention distributions across context that are attributable to specific architectural differences.

</details>


### [132] [Co-Evolution in Agentic Systems: Toward Self-Directed Evolution Beyond Human Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10299)
*Qing Zong,Jiayu Liu,Junhao Shen,Zecong Tang,Linsi Wu,Yuxuan Liu,Rui Wang,Zhaowei Wang,Weiqi Wang,Cheng Qian,Xiusi Chen,Yangqiu Song*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文综述了智能体系统中的协同进化（Co-Evolution）研究，提出一个三阶段递进式分类框架，用于理解多智能体及其环境如何相互施加适应性压力，从而突破静态学习上下文的限制，实现超越固定人工设计路径的开放进化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单一智能体的自进化常常受限于静态学习上下文（如固定任务和反馈），难以在部署后持续提升。作者认为需要从多组件协同进化的视角来突破这一瓶颈。

Method: 提出三阶段递进式分类法：(1) 智能体-智能体协同进化（对抗式、协作式、组织式适应）；(2) 智能体-环境协同进化（适应任务的动态变化、反馈与交互空间）；(3) 元协同进化（进化机制本身可进化）。同时讨论评估、扩展与安全性等开放挑战。

Result: 组织并梳理了现有协同进化相关论文，建立了统一的分类体系，并指出了评估、多组件扩展及自主进化安全控制等关键挑战。

Conclusion: 为构建稳健且开放式的智能体系统提供了统一的理论基础，使系统能够在固定人工设计路径之外持续自我改进。

Abstract: Agentic systems are increasingly expected to improve after deployment, yet single-entity self-evolution is often bounded by a static learning context, such as fixed tasks and feedback. This survey focuses on co-evolution in agentic systems, a multi-component form of self-evolution in which multiple agents and their environment impose adaptive pressure on one another. To organize existing papers, we propose a progressive three-stage taxonomy that traces how the system gradually sheds human-engineered constraints. Agent--Agent Co-Evolution studies how agents adapt through dynamic peers, including adversarial, collaborative, and organizational adaptation. Agent--Environment Co-Evolution extends this loop to adaptive tasks, feedback, and interaction spaces that change with the agents. Meta Co-Evolution further explores the possibility of making the evolution mechanism itself evolvable. We also discuss open challenges in evaluating such systems, scaling them across multiple components, and keeping increasingly autonomous evolutionary processes safe and controllable. This survey provides a unified foundation for building robust and open-ended agentic systems that can improve beyond fixed human-designed paths.

</details>


### [133] [Is This Your Final Answer? Cross-Contextual Consistency as a Measure of LLM Credibility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10315)
*Siyang Wu,Yibo Jiang,Bryon Aragam*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Cross-Contextual Consistency (C3)方法，通过比较同一任务在不同上下文扰动下的生成结果来衡量LLM回答的稳定性，发现跨上下文一致性越高，回答越可能正确。该方法在26个模型和6个基准上验证有效，可作为基准有用性诊断工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型是黑箱系统，难以判断其回答反映的是稳定的内部信念还是表面的模式匹配。现有评估方法缺乏对这种区分的能力，需要一种新的行为属性来帮助辨别。

Method: 提出Cross-Contextual Consistency (C3)方法，将同一任务置于主题对齐但内容中性的上下文变异下，比较模型在原始提示和扰动提示下的生成差异。差异越小，一致性越高。

Result: 在26个模型和6个基准（涵盖推理、事实性和代码生成）上的实验表明，跨上下文偏移较小的答案更可能是正确或事实性的。C3还能识别基准中即使聚合分数被认为"饱和"时仍具有信息量的部分。

Conclusion: C3为LLM评估提供了补充性维度，不仅可作为答案可信度的指标，还能作为基准有用性的诊断工具，帮助研究者在基准分数饱和的情况下仍能获得有意义的评估信息。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are powerful black-box systems, making it difficult to discern whether their answers reflect stable internal beliefs or superficial pattern matching. We identify cross-contextual consistency as an underutilized behavioral property of LLMs: a credible answer should remain stable when the same task is placed under topic-aligned, content-neutral contextual variation. Building on this intuition, we operationalize Cross-Contextual Consistency (C3) by comparing model generations under original and perturbed prompts. Across 26 models and six benchmarks spanning reasoning, factuality, and code generation, we find that answers with smaller cross-contextual shifts are more likely to be correct or factual. We demonstrate that C3 provides a complementary axis of evaluation and can serve as a benchmark usefulness diagnostic, identifying which portions of a benchmark remain informative even when aggregated scores are widely considered "saturate".

</details>


### [134] [VisEditBench: Can Vision-Language Models Edit Visualization Code from Multimodal Feedback?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10408)
*Mizanur Rahman,Arshia Azimlu,Shadikur Rahman,Md Tahmid Rahman Laskar,Amran Bhuiyan,Shafiq Joty,Enamul Hoque Prince*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出VisEditBench，一个包含1,395个人工标注的可视化代码编辑任务基准，涵盖反馈引导修复和参考引导重样式两种实际场景。通过评估20个SOTA视觉语言模型，发现可视化代码编辑仍具挑战性（最佳模型Claude-4.6-Sonnet仅74.46%通过率），并提出了基于渲染反馈的编辑框架VisEditAgent，将GPT-4o的通过率从55.75%提升至67.99%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实世界的可视化创建本质上是迭代的，用户经常需要修改现有可视化来修复有缺陷的图表或调整样式。然而现有基准主要评估从零开始的生成，缺乏对多模态反馈驱动的可视化代码编辑任务的系统性评估。

Method: 构建了1,395个基于真实工作流和失败案例的人工标注可视化代码编辑任务基准，覆盖两种设置：反馈引导修复（基于有缺陷/标记的图表加文本反馈进行修改）和参考引导重样式（修改代码以匹配目标图表图像）。同时提出VisEditAgent框架，通过迭代生成、执行、验证和细化候选编辑来利用渲染反馈改进编辑质量。

Result: 评估20个SOTA VLM显示可视化代码编辑仍具挑战性：Claude-4.6-Sonnet达到最佳总体通过率74.46%，多数开源模型低于50%；视觉引导样式适应的表现尤其薄弱（Claude-4.6-Sonnet仅55.71%）。VisEditAgent基于GPT-4o将总体通过率从55.75%提升至67.99%。

Conclusion: 可视化代码编辑对当前VLM仍是开放且困难的问题，尤其是视觉导向的样式适应任务。基于渲染反馈的迭代编辑框架能显著提升编辑忠实度，证明了渲染反馈在可视化编辑中的重要性。基准和代码将公开发布以推动该方向研究。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) have shown strong capabilities in generating visualization code from textual or visual specifications. However, real-world visualization authoring is inherently iterative: users frequently revise existing visualizations to repair flawed charts or adapt them to desired styles. Existing benchmarks primarily evaluate generation from scratch, leaving visualization code editing from multimodal feedback largely unexplored. We introduce VisEditBench, a benchmark of 1,395 human-annotated visualization code-editing tasks grounded in realistic visualization workflows and failure cases. VisEditBench covers two practical settings: feedback-guided repair, where models revise visualization code using buggy or marked charts together with textual feedback, and reference-guided restyling, where models modify code to match a target chart image. Evaluating 20 state-of-the-art VLMs reveals that visualization code editing remains challenging: Claude-4.6-Sonnet achieves the best overall pass rate of 74.46%, while most open-source models remain below 50%. Performance is particularly weak on visually grounded style adaptation, where Claude-4.6-Sonnet achieves only 55.71%. To establish a strong baseline, we further propose VisEditAgent, a render-grounded editing framework that iteratively generates, executes, validates, and refines candidate edits. Built on GPT-4o, VisEditAgent improves overall pass rate from 55.75% to 67.99%, demonstrating the importance of render-grounded feedback for faithful visualization editing. We will release VisEditBench at https://github.com/vis-nlp/VisEditBench.

</details>


### [135] [How Robust Are LLMs to Vietnamese Dialects?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10414)
*Minh Tran,Trinh Chau,Thanh-Nhan Le,Nam Tran,Luan Thanh Nguyen,Cuong Dang,Duc Hoang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文首次系统评估了LLM对越南语方言变体的鲁棒性，构建了包含400条标准越南语和2,400条六大方言改写句子的基准VialectBench，涵盖情感识别、自然语言推理、问答和多项选择问答四个任务。实验发现方言输入使十款指令微调模型性能平均下降2.82%，所有任务均受影响且没有模型完全方言不变，不同方言组之间鲁棒性差异显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有越南语方言研究主要关注方言到标准的归一化转换，而非衡量模型在方言输入下的失败表现；同时LLM通常仅在标准书面越南语上评估，忽略了日常沟通中常见的方言变异。

Method: 构建VialectBench基准数据集，包含400条标准越南语源实例和2,400条人工撰写的方言改写（覆盖6个方言组），涉及情感识别、自然语言推理、问答和多项选择问答四个任务；通过固定参考语言模型评估方言改写引起的模型相对似然偏移，并在十款指令微调模型上系统测量性能下降和失败模式。

Result: 方言输入使平均性能下降2.82%，没有模型完全方言不变；四个任务均受影响，其中QA降幅最大；鲁棒性因方言组而异：PNT3和PNT2分别造成6.17%和4.73%的最大平均性能下降，而PNB反而提升0.42%；中部方言组（PNT1-PNT4）的有害翻转率最高（6.54%）。

Conclusion: 在标准越南语上的强性能并不能保证在保持语义的区域方言变异下仍可靠运行，方言鲁棒性应作为LLM评估的重要维度。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are typically evaluated on standard written Vietnamese, yet everyday communication frequently involves regional dialects that preserve meaning but differ in surface form. Existing Vietnamese dialect work largely addresses this issue through dialect-to-standard normalization instead of measuring how the model fails under Vietnamese dialectal inputs. To address this gap, we present the first systematic evaluation of LLM robustness to Vietnamese dialect variation across multiple tasks, quantifying performance degradation and failure patterns. We introduce VialectBench (Vietnamese Dialects Benchmarking), a controlled benchmark for testing whether model decisions remain stable across six Vietnamese dialect groups. VialectBench contains 400 Standard Vietnamese source instances and 2,400 human-written dialectal rewrites spanning emotion recognition (ER), natural language inference (NLI), question answering (QA), and multiple-choice question answering (MCQA). Dataset evaluation with a fixed reference language model shows that the dialectal rewrites induce a measurable model-relative likelihood shift while remaining nearly equal in length to their Standard counterparts. Across ten instruction-tuned models, dialectal inputs reduce average performance by 2.82%, and no evaluated model is fully dialect-invariant. All four tasks are affected, with QA showing the largest average degradation. Robustness also varies substantially across dialect groups: PNT3 and PNT2 cause the largest average performance drops, at 6.17% and 4.73%, respectively, whereas PNB slightly improves average performance by 0.42%. The Central dialect group (PNT1-PNT4) also yields the highest average harmful-flip rate across all models, at 6.54%. These findings show that strong performance on Standard Vietnamese does not guarantee reliable behavior under meaning-preserving regional variation.

</details>


### [136] [From Reasoning Depth to Reasoning Breadth: Evaluating Multi-Point Associative Reasoning in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10444)
*Si'an Xie,Jiaxun Liu,Biao Yang,Wei Yuan,Fan Yang,Tingting Gao,Ming Wu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出MPAR-Bench，一个英汉双语基准，专门评估大语言模型的"推理广度"能力——即并行探索多个语义方向并整合线索的能力。通过多智能体线索生成和人工验证构建1000个测试项，实验发现现有模型在推理广度上表现脆弱，推理深度并不自动转化为推理广度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究高度关注LLM的推理深度（长链推理），但忽略了推理广度——即在多个语义方向上并行探索并整合线索的能力。这是一个未被充分探索且当前基准尚未覆盖的关键能力维度。

Method: 从合作游戏Just One中获得灵感，每个测试项要求模型从多个独立生成的语义多样化线索中恢复隐藏目标。构建了1000个测试项，采用多智能体线索生成管道、基于嵌入的多样性过滤和人工验证。评估使用精确匹配准确率、ANLS、嵌入相似度、推理轨迹验证以及四种扰动测试（线索遮蔽、顺序打乱、干扰项注入、多步线索）。

Result: 扰动使英语准确率降低9-18个百分点，中文降低5-12个百分点。思考模式提高了标准设置下的准确率（尤其英语），但并未持续降低对扰动的敏感性。案例分析表明延长的推理可能推翻最初正确的假设。

Conclusion: 更大的推理深度并不能自动带来稳健的推理广度能力，推理广度仍然是当前基准尚未充分覆盖的领域，需要专门的设计与评估来推动该方向的发展。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have made substantial progress on reasoning tasks that require increasingly long and complex inferential chains. This progress primarily reflects reasoning depth. A complementary and comparatively unexamined capability is reasoning breadth: exploring multiple semantic directions in parallel and integrating the resulting clues into one coherent answer. We introduce MPAR-Bench, a bilingual English-Chinese benchmark that isolates reasoning breadth through multi-point associative reasoning. Inspired by the cooperative game Just One, each item asks a model to recover a hidden target from several independently generated, semantically diverse clues. We construct 1,000 items using a multi-agent clue-generation pipeline, embedding-based diversity filtering, and human verification. Only the answer space is drawn from public word lists, whereas every clue set is generated from scratch. Beyond exact-match accuracy, we evaluate models using accuracy, ANLS, embedding similarity, reasoning-trace verification, and four perturbations: clue masking, order shuffling, distractor injection, and multi-step clues. Across evaluated models, perturbations reduce accuracy by 9-18 percentage points in English and 5-12 percentage points in Chinese. Thinking mode improves standard-setting accuracy, especially in English, but does not consistently reduce sensitivity to perturbations. Case-level analysis also shows that extended reasoning can overturn an initially correct hypothesis. These results indicate that greater reasoning depth does not automatically confer robust reasoning breadth, and that reasoning breadth remains largely uncovered by current benchmarks.

</details>


### [137] [MD-ProTector: Positioning Multiple Data-Driven Prototypes for LLM-Generated Text Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10459)
*Jinmo Han,Jimin Hong,Chanyeong Moon,Ju Yeon Kang,Seonuk Kim,Nam Soo Kim*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出MD-ProTector，一种基于多原型（multi-prototype）的编码器文本检测方法，通过可训练参考向量类内结构，并利用Prototype Positioning loss分离类级结构与类内变异，在多个大规模基准上取得最优检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM生成内容日益复杂，现有检测系统主要依赖标准二分类，只能输出类别标签，无法显式组织同一类别内部的多样变化（如不同写作风格、领域、语言、生成器模型）。这限制了检测系统的可解释性和泛化能力。

Method: MD-ProTector在编码器嵌入空间中为每个类别引入多个可训练参考向量（原型），为同类文本的不同分组提供独立决策边界；并提出Prototype Positioning loss，将类级结构与区分各原型的类内变异分离开来，从而解决多原型分配问题。

Result: 在三个大规模基准的五个设置（覆盖领域、生成器、语言和对抗性变异）中，MD-ProTector在MAGE CDCM和RAID上取得最高AvgRec，在RAID上取得最高AUROC和最低FPR95，超越了所有对比的编码器方法。

Conclusion: MD-ProTector通过多原型表征有效地组织类内变化，提升了LLM生成文本检测的性能，在多种变异场景下优于现有编码器检测方法。

Abstract: As LLM-generated content becomes more sophisticated, detection systems for distinguishing those texts from human-written text must operate at scale while handling diverse writing styles, domains, languages, and generator models. Input-only encoder detectors are suitable for practical deployment setting, but standard binary classification supplies only the class label and does not explicitly organize the substantial variation within either class. We propose MD-ProTector, which represents each class with multiple trainable reference vectors in the encoder embedding space, referred to as prototypes. These prototypes provide separate decision boundaries for different groups of texts within the same class. However, adding multiple prototypes alone does not determine which variation each prototype should represent. MD-ProTector addresses this problem with Prototype Positioning loss, which separates class-level structure from the within-class variation that differentiates individual prototypes. Evaluated across five settings from three large-scale benchmarks covering domain, generator, language, and adversarial variation, MD-ProTector achieves the highest AvgRec on MAGE CDCM and RAID and the highest AUROC and lowest FPR95 on RAID among the compared encoder-based methods.

</details>


### [138] [Calibrating Post-Training Feature Shifts for LLM Data Contamination Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10462)
*Zhen Yang,Mengqi Wang,Gengda Zhao,Mo Zhou,Jianwei Wang,Wenjie Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出CalibDCD框架，用于校准基于特征的训练数据污染检测方法，通过多视角偏移检测和有界特征校正来缓解后训练（如指令微调、偏好优化）对成员特征分离性的负面影响，实验显示AUC最高提升7.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于特征的DCD方法在多数LLM经过后训练（指令微调、偏好优化、推理训练）后，模型输出改变导致成员与非成员特征可分离性下降，检测性能受损。

Method: CalibDCD包含两个核心组件：(1) 多视角偏移检测——在已知非成员文本上评估受控提示变体，整合信息量最大的视角以识别后训练导致的重复特征偏移；(2) 有界特征校正——选择性调整与检测到的偏移对齐的特征分量，并控制校正程度以保留有用的检测信息。

Result: CalibDCD一致性地改进了现有基于特征的检测器，AUC最高提升7.0%，TPR@5%FPR最高提升15.0%。

Conclusion: 所提出的校准框架具有广泛的适用性，可有效缓解后训练对数据污染检测的负面影响，且无需修改底层检测器即可直接提升其性能。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are trained on massive and largely undisclosed corpora that may contain copyrighted or privacy-sensitive content. Data contamination detection (DCD) therefore aims to determine whether a given text is a member of the pre-training corpus of a target LLM. Recent state-of-the-art DCD methods follow a feature-based paradigm that derives membership features from the input text and the corresponding model output. However, most modern LLMs undergo post-training, such as instruction tuning, preference optimization, and reasoning-oriented training, which can alter model outputs and shift the corresponding membership features, thereby reducing the separability between members and non-members.
  To address this problem, we propose CalibDCD, a broadly applicable calibration framework for feature-based DCD methods, comprising (1) Multi-View Shift Detection, which identifies recurring feature shifts associated with post-training, and (2) Bounded Feature Correction, which selectively mitigates their influence on membership prediction. Specifically, Multi-View Shift Detection evaluates controlled prompt variants on known non-member texts and consolidates the most informative views to identify recurring feature shifts. Bounded Feature Correction selectively adjusts feature components aligned with the detected shifts and controls the correction extent to preserve useful detection information.
  Experiments show that CalibDCD consistently improves existing feature-based detectors, with gains of up to 7.0% in AUC and 15.0% in TPR@5%FPR.

</details>


### [139] [Every Token Counts: Exact Likert-Scale Distributions for Measuring LLM Attitudes and Biases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10503)
*Davood Wadi,Mohsen Ghodrat,Matthew Philp*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于LLM受控行为评估的解析精确框架，通过全因子实验设计替代非结构化提示，直接操作token级概率质量函数消除采样噪声，并推导多变量序数共识指标和分布性ANOVA进行解析处理。在五个LLM的消费民族中心主义案例研究中，证明了该方法能分离出聚合基准所掩盖的系统性原产国偏见。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的NLP社区通常使用大型非结构化基准评估LLM，这些数据集虽然能检测到聚合偏差，但无法厘清偏差究竟源于基线特征、情境混杂因素还是复杂交互作用，导致因果机制被混淆。作者旨在构建一个能够精确分离因果效应的受控评估框架。
